**v4.46 (2026-08-31) — Phase 3 stop gate ruled satisfied and `DEC-20260829-01` ratified.** The Founder ruled the Phase 3 stop-gate closure in `DEC-20260815-17` v0.30, discharging all three conditions the 2026-08-27 reconciliation ruling named: the five-capability convergence assessment is present on `main` with its five evidence-bound capability rows (`PHASE3-20260827-01` — its content, not its `status:` field, being the discharge fact); its five dispositions were ruled in `DEC-20260827-01` §3, ratified 2026-08-28; and the bounded reconciliation review landed as `DEC-20260829-01` at merge commit `4104527d37e9a28e926e0eb2134e336da4c24b67`. **The closure is ruled but not self-executing** — it becomes durable only on the ruling reaching `main` under a separate SHA-named merge authorization, which is what this record does. `DEC-20260829-01` is ratified `active` / `source_of_truth: true` at v0.7, artifact pin `ad7b6f445479775046b4616182eaa0847b2fbd93213d8add43f2b7ddf5128f33` (v0.6, byte-identical to the head `a4442307e8e318c1b4a0c90c9b3771178c7a8c06` that `gemini-3.1-pro` reviewed `PASS` with zero findings after five Tier-2 rounds across three model families). **Phase 4 is not authorized by this closure** and awaits its own `## Founder Authorization` section, matching every prior phase; `build-room-web`, Build Room CLI, TUI, MadBridge and Gateway implementation remain unauthorized. `DEC-20260827-01` §3.2's requirement that the command-journal disposition be resolved before any CONSUME ruling in that decision is implemented survives the closure unchanged. The rescinded 2026-08-26 closure conclusion stays rescinded and is retained as history. Freeze posture: `DEC-20260814-03` clause-3 record-keeping; no clause-4 exception claimed. decision-log v4.45 → v4.46.

**v4.45 (2026-08-30) — a fifth merge-path outcome recorded: correct trailers on a compliant PR body landed malformed on `main` because GitHub wrapped the merge-commit message; and a Founder-authorized line-length safeguard for future PRs.** Record-keeping for the incident; `main` is not rewritten. **What happened.** PR #316 merged at `24cd66a26e47ce803e7e8c2b2903a690606894b6`. Its PR body was verified correct immediately before merge — Attribution trailers end-anchored at column 0, `attribution-shape` green on the final head `2db2ab0`, Tier-2 `PASS-WITH-ADVISORIES` from `grok-4.5`. Post-merge verification against the merge SHA nevertheless returned `FAIL (c) final-merged-commit 24cd66a...: Role-Id present but Actor-Id missing`. **Cause, measured not inferred.** GitHub hard-wrapped the merge-commit message body. `Actor-Id: session:claude-code/dec-20260815-17-next-action-state-correction` is 74 characters and broke at its only space, leaving `Actor-Id:` alone on body line 273 and its value on line 274. The gate matches `^Actor-Id:[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]`; a colon followed by a newline has no non-space character on that line, so the trailer reads as absent while `Role-Id` is present — clause 19(c) fails. **What the measurement does and does not establish — two readings were corrected, the second by Tier-2 review.** An initial reading treated 73 as a demonstrated-safe source length, because no *wrapped* prose line in the landed body exceeds 73. That was wrong and is withdrawn: those 71-to-73-character lines are the wrapper's *output*, produced by breaking long paragraphs, not source lines observed to survive. A second claim — that no prose line in the landed body exceeds 73 at all — was also wrong, and is likewise withdrawn: body line 221 is an unfenced 97-character line. **That line reveals the mechanism.** The wrapper breaks at spaces, and a token containing no space cannot be broken; line 221 is a single backticked path with no space in it, so it overflowed the column intact. The `Actor-Id` line at 74 characters contained exactly one space — the separator after the colon — and broke precisely there. That is why this is the fatal case rather than a cosmetic one: every attribution trailer contains exactly one space, so an over-length trailer always splits its value onto the following line, which is the shape the gate reads as absent. **What is directly evidenced.** Five trailer lines survived unchanged at 16, 27, 30, 35 and 56 characters, the longest being `Tier2-Head-Sha:` at 56; one failed, the `Actor-Id` line at 74. Nothing between 57 and 73 is directly evidenced either way, and the observation cannot distinguish "wrap beyond 73" from "wrap at 72 with a word permitted to overhang to 73." Lines inside fenced code blocks are exempt and survived at up to 123 characters, but trailers cannot be fenced. **This is a new failure class, not a repeat.** `DEC-20260721-01` records that observed merge outcomes have varied by technique and that no merge path is established as safe; this instance adds a cause independent of technique — message *width*. PRs #314 and #315 passed post-merge verification with the identical merge path and identical trailer shape; their `Actor-Id` values were simply shorter. Nothing about how the merge was performed differed. **Why no gate could catch it.** The pre-merge `attribution-shape` run evaluates the PR body and the branch commits. The merge commit does not exist at that point, and its message is produced by GitHub at merge time. A green pre-merge gate therefore establishes nothing about the merge commit's trailers — the same shape-only limitation `DEC-20260721-01` clause 5 and `DEC-20260718-05` clause 20 already record, here reaching a surface neither clause named. **Founder ruling, 2026-08-30, recorded verbatim:** "FOUNDER RULING — 2026-08-30 — ATTRIBUTION TRAILER LINE-LENGTH SAFEGUARD. I authorize the proposed attribution trailer line-length safeguard as a narrow clause-2 process refinement notwithstanding the current freeze. The authorization is limited to preserving the existing attribution requirement across the GitHub merge path observed at merge commit `24cd66a26e47ce803e7e8c2b2903a690606894b6`. It creates no new attribution field, role, authority, approval requirement, merge authority, or exception class. For future PR attribution blocks, each attribution trailer source line must be **72 characters or fewer** unless later evidence establishes and Founder authority adopts a different bound. For `Actor-Id: `, this leaves a maximum value length of **62 characters**. The 72-character bound is a conservative operational safeguard derived from the measured failure on `24cd66a`: the 74-character `Actor-Id` source line was wrapped when GitHub produced the merge-commit message, causing the existing attribution check to read `Actor-Id` as missing. This ruling does not claim that 72 characters is a universal or permanent GitHub platform limit. Pre-merge verification cannot prove the final merge commit's attribution shape because that commit does not yet exist. It may, however, enforce this source-line-length safeguard before merge so known-overlength attribution lines do not enter the affected merge path. The malformed published merge commit remains unchanged. `main` must not be rewritten to repair its message. This is a bounded process refinement only. It does not invoke clause 4, does not identify or require a blocked ship, and does not otherwise relax the freeze." **Disposition: recorded, not repaired.** The malformed trailer stays visible on `24cd66a` as the evidence for this entry, consistent with this file's "log, don't rewrite" convention and with `CLAUDE.md`'s standing instruction to escalate rather than alter published history when post-merge verification fails. The substantive content of #316 landed correctly and is unaffected: `DEC-20260815-17` at v0.29, this file at v4.44, `path-audit` PASS and `dec-consistency-check` PASS at `24cd66a`. **Freeze posture.** This decision-log entry is `DEC-20260814-03` clause 3 record-keeping. The `CLAUDE.md` safeguard it records is a clause-2 process refinement expressly authorized by the Founder ruling above; no clause-4 shipping-blocker exception is claimed, identified, or required. decision-log v4.44 → v4.45.

**v4.44 (2026-08-30) — `DEC-20260815-17`'s `## Next Action` paragraph corrected: the five-capability assessment and its dispositions are no longer stated as absent/pending.** Founder-directed factual-state correction, executed as `builder`. **The defect.** The Next Action paragraph (lines 1278–1286 at `1abfa3c`) asserted "the active supplemental five-capability convergence assessment is absent" and "separate Founder rulings of its five dispositions" as pending. Both are false at current `main`: the assessment exists and is `status: active` at `/07-decisions/phase3-five-capability-convergence-assessment.md` (`PHASE3-20260827-01`, v0.5, `source_of_truth: false`), and the Founder ruled all five dispositions in `DEC-20260827-01` (v0.9, `source_of_truth: true`) — 3.1 CONSUME, 3.2 DEFER, 3.3 CONSUME, 3.4 CONSUME, 3.5 CONSUME, ratification recorded verbatim in its §8. Note 3.2 is DEFER, not CONSUME — the correction does not state all five as consumed. The third condition remains true and unclosed: the bounded reconciliation review under `DEC-20260820-01` clause 4 is commissioned by `HO-20260829-01` and not yet produced as `DEC-20260829-01`. Phase 3 therefore stays **open**, on that one remaining condition rather than three. **Invariant preserved, checked, not assumed.** The paragraph's three governing prohibition sentences ("No Phase 4 work, deployment, run, gateway action, configuration change, new spend, or MVP-status change is authorized."; "`build-room-web` remains outstanding and gains no authority."; "No Build Room CLI, TUI, MadBridge, or Gateway implementation work is authorized by this reconciliation.") were required to survive with their wording unchanged — verified up to whitespace normalization, not raw byte identity. A line-level diff would report a false difference regardless of care taken, because the first prohibition sentence begins mid-line in the source and any rewrap of the preceding sentence shifts it — so the check was run at the sentence level instead: both pre- and post-edit paragraphs extracted, newlines collapsed and whitespace normalized to single spaces, then each of the three sentences confirmed present verbatim in both. All three passed. That check's own blind spot: it is a substring match, so it would return a false pass if a sentence's exact text were spliced into a new, meaning-altering surrounding clause without changing the sentence's own characters — it confirms the text survived, not that it remains a free-standing, unqualified sentence, and — because the comparison is whitespace-normalized — not that its internal whitespace is unchanged; the post-edit paragraph was read directly to confirm it is not so embedded. **`dec-consistency-check.py` FAILED on first run, then PASSED after a fix.** The v0.29 frontmatter bump initially had no matching `## Notes` entry (`FAIL: versions with no Notes entry: ['0.29']`); fixed by adding a v0.29 Notes entry in house style next to the existing v0.28 entry, after which the same check returned `PASS — no self-referential contradiction detected`. Both outputs are preserved in the carrying PR body rather than showing only the final pass. **Frontmatter.** `DEC-20260815-17` v0.28 → v0.29, `updated` → 2026-08-30, `related_decisions` gains `DEC-20260827-01-phase3-five-capability-convergence-dispositions` and `DEC-20260820-01-madventures-tui-governance-binding` — the Next Action now turns on both and previously pointed at neither, the same unresolvable-pointer class the v4.43 entry above closed for `DEC-20260827-01`. No ratified clause outside the named paragraph was touched; no operative disposition reopened or restated; the retained CR1→CR2→CR3 evidence and the assessment/`DEC-20260827-01` files themselves were not edited. **Freeze posture, as ruled:** not clause-2 frozen — this creates no governance machinery, no process refinement, and no new rule; it corrects a factual state claim. `DEC-20260814-03` clause 3 covers the version bump, `updated` field, and this decision-log row as record-keeping. No clause-4 shipping-blocker exception is claimed or needed. **Not merge authorization:** `/07-decisions/` is a `DEC-20260814-02` Tier-2 trigger surface; the carrying PR merges only after independent Tier-2 review of the final exact head and the Founder's separate SHA-named authorization. This entry was applied by the Founder directly, not by the `builder` agent session — the `decision-log.md` authority-path control blocks agent writes to this file by design (confirmed by three identical `PreToolUse` denials across three separate attempts), so the `builder` commit carries `DEC-20260815-17-phase-sequencing.md` alone and this entry lands as a separate Founder commit on the same branch; the carrying PR therefore changes two files, split by authorship at that boundary, not one. decision-log v4.43 → v4.44.

**v4.43 (2026-08-30) — `DEC-20260827-01`'s evidence base made resolvable in both directions; and this log's own frontmatter/narrative desync recorded, not repaired.** Record-keeping only: no decision, disposition, ruling, decision authority, or operative status changed, and no row was added to the Active decisions table. One non-authoritative bookkeeping status does change and is described below — the assessment's own `proposed` -> `active` frontmatter value, flagged for Founder disposition rather than ruled (`DEC-20260827-01`'s row already exists). Founder-directed after the Founder surfaced the assessment file directly. **The finding.** `DEC-20260827-01` ruled five dispositions against an evidence base it cites only in prose — Sections 6 and 7 say "the assessment", cite PR #304, and quote a line range within it (`L160–163`), but never name the artifact by path or by its `PHASE3-20260827-01` identifier; the assessment, for its part, listed `DEC-20260820-01` and `DEC-20260815-17` in `related_decisions` but not the decision that consumed every one of its rows. Neither file could be reached from the other without already knowing the PR number, and this log cited the assessment's path zero times. Not a false statement in either file — a missing pointer, the same class as (though a much weaker instance than) the `DEC-20260820-01` C-3 zero-hit finding, where a citation had formed with no supporting record at all. **Executed** on branch `docs/dec20260827-crossref`: `DEC-20260827-01` v0.7 → v0.8 gains a `## Notes` section placed after Section 9 so no ratified operative text is touched, recording the artifact's full path, identifier, frontmatter class and three bound repository heads; the assessment v0.4 → v0.5 gains the reciprocal `related_decisions` entry and a consumed-on note stating which disposition each row received and that the decision, not the assessment, is binding authority. **Evidence pins re-verified 2026-08-30, not assumed — and a first-draft error in that verification corrected before merge:** all thirteen Section 5 consumed-interface paths have zero commits between bound head `7d37a61dcaeaef77c55013c7620deb8445726788` and `madventures-tui` `6f2994c509fcfedc74f3784a558d06bf4a3c3ba2` (named check, per path, both endpoints pinned as full SHAs so a replay cannot drift: `git log <bound>..<head> -- <path>`). Beyond Section 5, the assessment's wider path inventory took **six** independent-review rounds to state correctly — four Greptile P1s on PR #314, a Tier-2 FAIL from `grok-4.5`, and a CodeRabbit Major — and the sequence is the finding, not the final number. The v0.8 draft said "fourteen MadBridge source paths" and claimed all fourteen were verified in `madventures-tui`. Every element of that was wrong. Only ten were MadBridge; the rest were Build Room paths that **do not exist in `madventures-tui` at all**, so the zero-commit results there were **vacuous** — the range was empty because the paths were absent, not because nothing changed — and a vacuous result had been written into a verification record as evidence. Four successive figures were then published and each superseded — 14, then 19, then 21, now **22** — and the through-line is not arithmetic: every figure came from a matcher carrying an *undisclosed restriction*, so each number looked like a census while actually reporting whatever that matcher admitted. Fourteen missed five `packages/run-harness/src/phase3/*` files because the enumerating pattern's character class excluded digits; nineteen was scoped to `packages/**` and omitted `apps/madbridge/src/commands/export-evidence.ts`; both deduplicated by path *string*, collapsing `packages/ledger/src/ledger.ts` — which the assessment cites twice with two different blobs, one per repository — into a single MadBridge entry; and twenty-one still required a `.ts` extension, omitting `docs/phase3-counted-run-harness.md` (blob `f7b019cf5d19fa61ad2f1cdc1e378b6b4d731952`), a Build Room artifact the assessment cites with a blob exactly as it cites the source files. That last one was raised as Tier-2 FAIL finding F1 by `grok-4.5` against head `2ed30f7`. All six rounds are one defect class: **a stated scope narrower than the claim it was offered as evidence for**, the reader-side mirror of the shape-only limitation `DEC-20260721-01` clause 5 records for the gates. Because a fifth undisclosed filter followed four corrections of the same kind, the remedy at `DEC-20260827-01` v0.9 is not a fifth number but a published extraction rule plus a resolution method. **Extraction rule:** every backtick-quoted path the assessment pairs with a parenthesised `blob` and a 40-hex SHA, with no filter on directory, extension, or file kind. **Resolution:** each artifact is a **(repository, path) pair resolved by blob identity** — `git rev-parse BOUND_HEAD:THE_PATH` run in *both* repositories, assigning the artifact to whichever returns exactly the cited blob. **Round six then found the same defect class inside that remedy**, which is the strongest available evidence that stating a method's conditions, not restating its output, is what closes this class: the draft claimed a zero-commit `git log BOUND_HEAD..CURRENT_MAIN -- THE_PATH` range "cannot be vacuous", but that holds only if the bound head is an ancestor of the current head — an unstated, unchecked condition, since a rewritten or diverged `main` could return an empty range while the path there was absent or different. Raised by CodeRabbit (Major) against head `4f6e585`; the claim is withdrawn. **The range is no longer the drift evidence.** Each artifact is now compared directly at the current head — `git rev-parse CURRENT_MAIN:THE_PATH` must return exactly the cited blob — which is strictly stronger, needs no ancestry assumption, and cannot be satisfied by a missing path; ancestry is separately verified with `git merge-base --is-ancestor` and recorded as fact rather than assumed. On that basis the assessment cites **22 unique (repository, path) artifacts — 11 MadBridge, 11 Build Room** — across 21 path strings and 24 path-plus-blob mentions; all 22 resolved unambiguously to one repository, and **all 22 are byte-identical at their repository's current `main`**, zero mismatches and zero absences. Section 5 was never affected — it is MadBridge-only and its thirteen paths were always checked in the correct repository; the earlier caveat that only three Section 5 blobs were verified byte-exact and the remaining ten by commit-drift alone is superseded, since every cited artifact is now verified by content. M18/M19 (Tasks 40–43) landed entirely under `packages/pty-host/` and touched none of the five assessed capabilities. **Flagged for Founder disposition, not ruled:** the assessment's `status` moves `proposed` → `active`, labelled in the file itself as a builder bookkeeping proposal rather than a ruling; `active` follows the `type: research` precedent in this repo, `approved` is also in the frontmatter-standard enum and was not chosen unilaterally, and reverting the change alters no authority. **Recorded rather than repaired — this log's own numbering.** This file's frontmatter reached `version: 4.41` at `9f2e8d1` (the `DEC-20260827-01` dispositions/reservation/log-rows commit) and `4.42` at `80fdd31` (the ratification), but **no `v4.41` or `v4.42` Current Status entry was ever written** — verified by `git log -S"**v4.41 ("` and `-S"**v4.42 ("` over this file's full history, zero commits each. That is a frontmatter/narrative desync, not the merge-collision class this file already records for `v3.72`, `v2.61/v2.62`, `v4.35/v4.36` and `v4.37/v4.38`, where entries existed and were overwritten. Per this file's own "log, don't rewrite" convention the gap is left visible and the numbering continues from the frontmatter: this entry is **v4.43**, and the narrative jump from `v4.40` is the gap, described rather than closed. Whether to backfill the two missing entries is a Founder call. **Not merge authorization:** `/07-decisions/` is a `DEC-20260814-02` Tier-2 trigger surface; the carrying PR merges only after independent Tier-2 review of the final exact head and the Founder's separate SHA-named authorization. Freeze posture: `DEC-20260814-03` clause 3 record-keeping — creates no rule, grants no authority, claims no clause-4 shipping-blocker exception, and selects no disposition. decision-log v4.42 → v4.43.

**v4.33 (2026-08-25) — Founder ruling: root `/CLAUDE.md` frontmatter `type:` is `system`, not `agent`; executed the same day.** Ruling requested via `HO-20260825-01` (opened after CodeRabbit flagged missing frontmatter on PR #291, then corrected to cite `04-agents/claude-code.md`'s existing `type: agent` precedent as a real but non-settling option).
